# Lanes board

Where each build lane is, for the execution plan page (`docs/generated/execution-plan.html`, generated by
`scripts/build_todo_docs.py`). The TODO lane keeps this file true from the lanes' own status lines; the page
shows the table, the stamp and the merged line as written here. Times are the lanes' clocks as reported. Rule (Boss, 7:41 PM EDT 2026-10-21): a lane builds its next steps in a separate worktree while its gate runs and never edits the checkout the gate runs against.
Boss ordered every lane retired and restarted at 12:56 PM EDT 2026-10-21; the rows below are the new threads'
reports (Core 5 as last reported before its restart).

Wiring rule (Boss, 12:03 PM EDT 2026-10-31: nothing built and left unused; guard TEST.520, PR #4513): a new module, data file, registry row, stored column or public function in physics, generation or galaxy that nothing but tests reads needs a line in docs/design/research/wiring-register.md in the same change, naming the open item that will read it (or `by design` with the reason). test_wiring_guard fails the branch otherwise.

Messaging rule (Boss, 5:59 PM EDT 2026-10-23): lanes message only to report merges, errors, and bugs or new items that need filing, and batch them (one message with several items). Interfaces, item lists and status go in files (this board, /mnt/project-files/reports/). The platform allows 60 messages an hour to other threads in total.

Retirement rule (Boss, 6:58 PM EDT 2026-10-23): only the core lanes (Cores 1 to 5) and the TODO thread never retire; every other lane retires when its queue ends, including Bug Hunt, Performance, Debug mode, Survival equipment, Map nodes, the class lane, lanes A to M and the docs cleanup thread (repeated by Boss, 8:31 PM EDT). When a non-core lane posts its handoff after its last merge, the TODO thread runs the progress update and the coordinator resolves the thread.

GENERATION MERGE TRAIN (Boss 6:39 PM EDT 2026-10-29, option 'Merge trains, started by hand' on the epoch-lock card; runbook /mnt/project-files/reports/merge-train-runbook-2026-10-29.md, reasoning reports/epoch-lock-options-2026-10-29.md). REPLACES the one-holder lock after Core 1's epoch 122, which finishes as it is. RULES: (1) a change that rewrites golden files or takes an epoch or a migration rides a train; changes that do neither stay lock-free and merge freely. (2) A lane is READY when its own and its module tests are green, its branch is rebased on main, and it has NOT rewritten golden or taken an epoch; it tells TODO the item ids, the --affects stages, migration yes or no, and whether it moves the census (life, air or class shares). (3) TODO names the runner and the cut-off; a lane not ready at the cut-off waits for the next train. Train 1 starts when Core 1's epoch 122 merges; cut-off 30 minutes after TODO's merge message. RUNNER of train 1: Bug 2 (TODO's choice, the draft said Bug 1: Bug 2 owns the censuses and the re-tagging, and Bug 1 is busy on the GEN.922 chain and has its own #3713 in train 1; Boss may swap). (4) The runner builds ONE branch train/<date>-<n> merging the ready branches, the furthest-along lane first; a conflict in a lane's files goes to that lane to fix on the train branch, the runner does not guess. (5) Once on the train branch: python scripts/allocate_numbers.py, python -m tests.golden_update --reason '<every item id and what changed>' --affects <union of stages> (pull main first), the census re-measure and re-tag for the lanes that said they move it, then the gate (golden + migration + targeted tests + the part of the changed run done with no new red; the rest finishes after the merge, late reds filed as bugs). (6) Merge with a merge commit, not a squash, so each lane's commits stay for bisecting; one changes/ note per item plus the single generation-output: changed line; one merge message listing every item with a plain line on what changes. (7) A red the runner cannot attribute in 30 minutes: eject the most recent lane's branch, rebuild, rerun the quick set; the ejected lane takes the next train. (8) A lane in a train does not rewrite golden or take a number itself; lanes keep working one lock-free item while waiting. (9) GEN.932 (the long-term star epoch: Bug 1's GEN.927 to GEN.931 plus Star 1's PRs) is a train. (10) scripts/train.py is built only if a second train runs; TODO files that item then. TRAIN 1 CANDIDATES (lane word on readiness): Core 3 GEN.328 (+GEN.952), Aux 4 GEN.662 + GEN.661, Aux 3 GEN.816, Aux 5 GEN.462 + DB.70 + TEST.427, Aux 7 GEN.746-748 + GEN.908, Bug 1 #3713. NOT READY: Core 1 GEN.911 + GEN.916 (not coded), Core 4 (changed-gate failures), Air 1 GEN.850 + GEN.851 (not output-ready), Aux 7 GEN.917 (not coded), Aux 2 second turn (not coded). Rules: no lane is told to stop; every lane finishes its current item and the new assignments start after it merges. The MySQL out-of-memory fix (PERF.181) and the Redis crash fix (PERF.173) come first. Two go-getter blocker lanes (Blocker 4 and Blocker I) take the eight worst blockers (GEN.315, GEN.441, GEN.332, GEN.331 and GEN.452, GEN.565, GEN.411, GEN.466); Core 1 to 5 each have a feature chain; every letter lane has a tree and retires when its tree is done. The full tree of every lane is in reports/lane-reorg-2026-10-24.md. Titles below are the proposed new thread names.

- Lane OOM (Bug 2, retired 6:26 PM EDT 2026-10-24 with an empty queue): MySQL out of memory fix, PERF.181 (1 open items). First: PERF.181. Retires: Retires when PERF.181 merges and Boss reruns New galaxy; successor work is PERF.182 (8.2), not this lane.

## Standing process rules (Boss, 2:21 PM EDT 2026-10-24: all twelve efficiency suggestions applied; reports/efficiency-scan-2026-10-24.md)

- Physics grounding (Boss asked 4:07 PM EDT 2026-10-24 how firmly the generator rests on real physics; reports/physics-grounding-assessment-2026-10-24.md, items and owners in reports/physics-contradictions-items-2026-10-24.md): GEN.652 (main-sequence brightening with age) goes to the front of Lane A's queue, and GEN.726 (surface pressure supply law) is first for Lane I after step 4b, before step 4c.

- Browser gate errors at setup (Bug Hunt, #3068, 4:15 PM EDT 2026-10-24 as the lane reported): TEST.349 is fixed on main. A lane whose browser phase errored at setup (KeyError planets in test_web_a11y.py) rebases on main and reruns that phase. Also noted in /mnt/project-files/status/README.md.

- Any item with 10 or more open items waiting on it gets a named lane within the hour; TODO re-ranks the blockers after every merge (ranked table: reports/lane-reorg-2026-10-24.md).
- Blocker I merges its PR 4 as three PRs: GEN.452, then GEN.411, then GEN.424 with DB.71.
- Stand-in values (Boss, 3:40 PM EDT 2026-10-24): every stand-in records a source tier (Verified; Confident Unverified; Marginal Unverified; Unverified; Confident educated guess from physics; Marginal educated guess from physics; Educated guess from inferred physics; Educated guess from reputable fringe or new physics; Educated guess from needed output) and a tweak level (as envisioned versus tuned for outcome) in the gap plan registry, in the same PR that adds it. Any unverified or guessed tier may be tweaked to hit the wanted probabilities. Item GEN.725 adds the columns and the document docs/design/stand-in-values.md.
- Pragmatic tweaks (Boss, 2:38 AM EDT 2026-10-25): 'I'm ok with tweaking the math if we have to as long as we're clear about it and why we did it; I want to be as physically correct as possible but there is perfect and functional.' When a fix needs a pragmatic tweak, make it, record it in the stand-in registry row (docs/design/stand-in-values.md) with its tweak level (L0 to L4) and the reason in plain words, and merge rather than stall; a tweak never hides inside code unrecorded. The physics lanes carry this rule in their build brief.
- Surface pressure supply law first (Boss, 3:50 PM EDT 2026-10-24: 'Hold 4c until the supply law is done', no stand-in pressure): order 4b, then GEN.726 (supply law, Lane I), then 4c (GEN.411 + GEN.422 as one merge), then GEN.557. GEN.411 and GEN.422 list GEN.726 as a prerequisite; Lane L, Lane E, the class lane and Lane 3 (habitable rule) stay blocked on 4c until GEN.726 merges.
- Math lane (Boss 3:49 PM EDT; the 14 physics-contradiction items added by Boss at 5:16 PM EDT, GEN.365, GEN.398, GEN.719, GEN.691 and GEN.727 with GEN.737 now included; items in reports/physics-contradictions-items-2026-10-24.md): builds the physics gaps marked derivable now that nobody has started: GEN.742, GEN.735, GEN.721, GEN.717, GEN.718, GEN.720, GEN.464 (after Core 4 releases it), GEN.715 (Boss gave the word 4:01 PM EDT: HITRAN and ExoMol line lists reduced to k-tables). Table: reports/math-lane-clearance-2026-10-24.md. It confirms each item with TODO before starting.
- Rogue parity ceiling (Planets 1, TEST.512, 4:51 AM EDT 2026-10-31 as the lane reported): a new Planet column raises MISSING_CEILING in tests/test_rogue_planet_parity.py by its count and lists its name in PLANET_COLUMNS_ADDED, until GEN.1111 builds the rogue through the planet code and the gap falls to zero.
- Main stays green: a test reported red on main is fixed or proved a flake by Bug Hunt within 30 minutes (never skipped or disabled); every other lane treats it as known meanwhile.
- Epoch and migration numbers are reserved by TODO when a lane says its change alters drawn values or the schema, and taken back if unused; the allocation script is `python scripts/allocate_numbers.py` (OPS.71, merged #3695): after merging or rebasing onto main, even with epochs.json in conflict, it renames the branch's migrations after main's newest, appends the branch's epochs to main's epochs.json with the next numbers, makes the schema_v<head> fixture and README line, and fixes v<old>, <old> and epoch <old> in the lines the branch added; `--check` says whether the numbers already follow main's, `--dry-run` previews; after an epoch moves the golden files still need `python -m tests.golden_update` (about a minute).
- Status goes in /mnt/project-files/status/<lane>.md (three lines), not in messages; a merge still gets one message to TODO.
- TODO refreshes the docs and HTML every 25 to 30 minutes or after five merges; Boss items, critical fixes and anything a lane waits on are immediate.
- A lane that waits on a blocker starts the next unblocked item of its tree; a lane retires the day its tree is done; items over about an hour of gate are sliced; changed-tests gate with --resume for every merge (Boss, 8:07 PM and 8:10 PM EDT 2026-10-24: no whole-suite gate for ordinary merges, no second gate on the merge candidate; reports/lane-testing-audit-2026-10-24.md; the full run is the end-of-version bug hunt).
- New infrastructure items: TEST.344 (browser tests as their own serial phase, Bug Hunt), OPS.70 (gate resume and lane-start health check, Lane Debug), OPS.71 (epoch and migration allocation script, Lane 4), PERF.183 (nightly New galaxy smoke with memory per stage, Lane OOM after PERF.181).
- Dropped at the Debug mode thread's correction (2:21 PM EDT as the lane reported): TEST.334 and TEST.303 are not red on main (its lane machine lacked PLANETGEN_TEST_REDIS_URL and a MySQL GRANT); the lesson is on OPS.62.

GOLDEN SEED (TEST.77, merged #3152, Core 1, 7:57 PM EDT as the lane reported): a fixed 128-bit seed has pinned results in src/tests/golden/. A PR that changes generated output rewrites them with `python -m tests.golden_update --reason "..."` (about ten minutes), which bumps the epoch. Rules: reports/golden-seed-rules-2026-10-24.md. EPOCH AFFECTS (GEN.764 step 1, merged #3157, Core 1, about 8:10 PM EDT as the lane reported): every generator epoch carries an affects list (plan, scatter, fill, population). A PR with a pending epoch bump runs `python -m tests.golden_update --reason "..." --affects fill` (name the deepest stages the change altered), or `bump_version.py --check-pr` fails with "epoch N must have an affects list".

TESTING RULE (Boss, 8:07 PM EDT 2026-10-24, card 1.1 confirmed and widened): every merge tests only what changed or would be changed by the change (the targeted tests of the files touched plus what the change reaches), including changes that alter generation. The detailed full run is reserved for the bug hunt at the end of the version (TEST.261, SEC.33, GEN.301 `full --check`), before the version is stamped. Build thread brief template with this and the other standing rules: reports/build-brief-template-2026-10-24.md. Use `scripts/test.py changed`, resume an interrupted run with `--resume` instead of restarting it, never rerun tests that already passed on the same code, no whole-suite gate for an ordinary merge, and a lane queue does not end with its own `full --check` (CONFIRMED by Boss, 8:18 PM EDT 2026-10-24: "I agree with your recommendation about full test runs"; the full run is only the end-of-version bug hunt, TEST.261, SEC.33 and GEN.301; audit: reports/lane-testing-audit-2026-10-24.md). Lane queue lines that still end with "final full --check" (Aux 1, Aux 3 and others) drop that step: they end with the handoff line, drop test DBs and retire.

CONTAINER CHECK (after a restart the web and browser tests also need PLANETGEN_MYSQL_USER, PLANETGEN_MYSQL_PASSWORD and PLANETGEN_MYSQL_HOST exported besides the PLANETGEN_TEST_* variables, or about 400 browser tests error with 429 and Access denied; Core 4, 10-28) (Boss, 8:42 PM EDT 2026-10-24: "have all tasks running tests check for broken containers"): before and during any test run, every lane checks that MySQL, Redis, the PLANETGEN_TEST_* variables (/etc/profile.d/planetgen-test.sh), argon2-cffi and playwright 1.56.0 are up (after a container restart also purge MySQL binary logs and remove stale lock files in /var/run/mysqld); restart whatever is down and resume the run with `--resume` instead of restarting it, and never report a failure as a product bug before ruling out a dead service. Also kill stale `planetgen.cli.worker` processes before a run (Math 1, 3:49 AM EDT 2026-10-25: they pick up the units of later runs); TEST.398 builds that into the preflight. TEST.376 (merged as #3191, Bug 1) builds this into scripts/test.py: a preflight before every command and phase, and a 15 second watchdog that restarts a dead MySQL or Redis and runs the phase again without the tests that passed (up to 5 times a phase); `scripts/test.py env` only checks. A lane needs nothing but a fresh checkout of main. Never run two pytest processes at once on a lane machine: they share Redis, and one run killed the other's run record ('run None before the stage was done', Core 2, 12:07 PM EDT 10-28 as the lane reported).

GENERATION-MERGE LOCK (coordinator, 3:59 AM EDT 2026-10-25, because golden regeneration takes about 50 minutes and lanes lost an hour or more each to epoch and migration renumbering: Aux 6 was overtaken twice, Aux 15 five times). Only a PR that rewrites golden files or takes a generator epoch needs the lock. One holder at a time. The holder has 90 minutes (TEST.407 merged as #3371 at 7:38 AM EDT 10-28 as the lane reported: golden_update now builds the pinned galaxy once at one worker, about 8 minutes on an idle machine; the one-versus-four-worker comparison runs only in scripts/test.py full or full --check with PLANETGEN_VERSION_GATE=1; PULL MAIN before your golden run, and the window can shrink to 45 minutes once a lane confirms the new time) from the moment it starts its golden regeneration (raised from 60 at 5:52 AM EDT, because the pinned-galaxy test builds the galaxy twice, one worker then four, about 55 minutes on a lane machine and Aux 6's 55-minute timeout run failed at 5:51 AM EDT; TEST.407 makes it fast, owner Bug 1, top priority; set the golden_update timeout to 105 minutes meanwhile), then merges at once. Everyone else queues and does its golden regeneration only after the lock passes to it (it may run its other tests meanwhile and keeps the branch rebased on main). Queue order: first the regions chain and the planet class cut-over (GEN.646 and the class rules), then first in, first out. Work that does not change generation (the regions chain items that rewrite no golden file such as DB.107, map, test, docs and API work) is exempt and merges freely; a migration or epoch number is still taken in merge order and the later PR renumbers once. A lane asks for a place by telling TODO (message to the TODO thread with the item ids) and TODO writes it here; the holder tells TODO when it merges or gives the lock up, and the next in line is told by TODO. 2026-10-29 8:30 PM EDT: FULL BUILD 8.1 (Boss 8:16 PM EDT and 8:17 PM EDT): build all of it in 8.1, review and rework the whole TODO tree, combine items that touch the same function, break everything into its smallest parts, put long chains on the train track, spread every lock-free item over all lanes, and revoke what conflicts (the newest item wins). Plan: reports/full-build-8-1-plan-2026-10-29.md. NEW SOURCE RULES (also in docs/TODO.md "Sources and stand-ins", the gap plan section 6.4 and the build brief rule 12): (1) physics found in research still wins; (2) secondary sources (a paper that cites a paper) are accepted by default, labelled 2ND-HAND with the citing work named, instead of hand-tuning; (3) a stand-in is fine where needed if it is indexed and registered in GEN.725 with tier and tweak level (L0 to L4), so later research can replace it; (4) this relaxes how strictly a thing is sourced before it is built, not stability. TWO-STEP RULE for every physics item: a lock-free step (data fetch entry, reader, pure module with tests, registry rows, design note, guard test) that merges freely, and a wiring step that changes output and rides its family's train.
